Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the fundamental_group framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 243. The resulting operator achieves bound O(782/n^4).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by fundamental_group generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 782. QED.

Statement: For any fundamental_group module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 712. The projective resolution has length at most 249 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_712(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 712. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^249(S, F) is trivial. QED.
